## Jason (BE) 2026-11-09 — TASK-879: Silver's 2 review conditions DONE (formula-injection guard + /security-review)
**1. Excel formula injection — guarded.** New `formulaSafe()` in `lib/slip-sections.ts`: a text cell that BEGINS with `=`, `+`, `-`, `@`, a tab or a CR gets a leading apostrophe, so a spreadsheet shows it as text and never runs it. Applied to every text cell of the XLSX (person name, period, status, section titles) and, because it had the same hole, to the **teacher-name column of the existing salary CSV export** (`salary.service.ts` `exportCsv`). Money cells are real numbers and never go through it (a negative subtotal stays a number). The PDF keeps labels as typed (not a spreadsheet). Note on where the admin's text lands: an admin's Entries reason is a LINE ITEM label, which appears only in the PDF; the Excel summary carries section titles and the person's name (from `public.teachers`, also person-typed), so those are the cells that needed the guard.
**Tests (+5, in `slip-export-task879.test.ts`):** the guard on `= + - @ \t \r` (incl. a `HYPERLINK` payload) and untouched ordinary/Thai/`a=b`/`x-1`/` =spaced` text; the XLSX name cell for `=1+1`, `@SUM(A1)`, `+cmd`, `-1+2` is a String cell with the apostrophe and has no formula; money cells stay numbers; the PDF keeps `=1+1 reason` unchanged; the CSV name column is guarded. **Mutation set now 18/18 bite** (added: xlsx name unguarded, `@` dropped from the guard, csv name unguarded).
**2. /security-review (TASK-879 whole):** none open after the fix above. Checked: both routes CEO-only behind the existing `ceo` guard (admin/coach 403, none 401, tested) with a UUID path id (malformed ⇒ 404); the filename is built from year, month and 8 hex characters of the id, so no header injection; responses are `no-store` attachments with fixed content types; fonts are read from embedded assets, never a request-supplied path; exceljs/pdfkit only WRITE files here (no parsing of untrusted input); person-typed text in the PDF is drawn as glyphs, not interpreted; the PDF `Title` metadata holds the person's name (not executable). Residual, accepted: pay data is in the file by design, and the CEO is the only reader.

## Jason (BE) 2026-11-09 — TASK-879 PDF fix DONE (subtotals of 1,000+ stay on one line)
**Cause:** the PDF's right-hand amount column was a fixed 110 pt; the bold 13 pt section header "Subtotal 20,800.00 ฿" is wider, so pdfkit wrapped it onto a second line. **Fix (`slip-export.service.ts` `row()`):** the amount column now measures its own text (`widthOfString + 6`, never less than 110) and the label column takes the rest; the amount is drawn with `lineBreak:false`, so it can never wrap. Applies to subtotals, line amounts and the total.
**Test (+1):** a slip with subtotals of 1,000.00 up to 1,234,567.89 and a negative one; every `Subtotal <amount> ฿`, every line amount and the total is a SINGLE extracted text line, and there is never a bare "฿" or a lone "Subtotal". It **bites**: with the old fixed-width layout put back, the test fails. (The test extractor now has `pdfRaw` for per-line text; `pdfText` is unchanged.)
**Evidence:** pinned tsc 5.9.3 → 0 `error TS` · backoffice-back suite 857 pass / 0 fail · `bun run build` 0 · `tests/evidence/task-879/sample-slip.pdf` and `.xlsx` regenerated from the same invented fixture (Pete) with the new layout. ## Jason (BE) 2026-11-09 — TASK-896 GREEN (test renamed + placeholder-token guard)
**Part 1 (record):** `src/middleware/service-auth-task890.test.ts` → `service-auth-task896.test.ts` (plain file rename, tags `TASK-890` → `TASK-896`, token literals `svc-890` → `svc-896`); no logic change. *Disclosure: I first tried `git mv -k` for the rename; it was a no-op (the file is untracked) and changed nothing, then I used a plain `mv`.*
**Part 2 (hardening):** `middleware/auth.ts` gains `PLACEHOLDER_SERVICE_TOKENS = ["change-me-service-token"]` (the one list to extend) and `configuredServiceToken()` = the env token, or `undefined` when it is unset, empty or a placeholder. **My call (you left it open): reject per request (401), not refuse at startup**, because a startup refusal would also break dev boxes that legitimately run with `SKIP_ADMIN_AUTH=true` and the placeholder. Every machine-auth check now goes through that one function: `serviceAuth` (accounts debits, recurring, teacher-sync), `adminOrService` (stock movements and sales; it accepted the placeholder as a machine token too) and Bob's `strictServiceToken` in `routes/salary-internal.ts` (`/internal/salary/unconfirmed`; a one-line import + call change, tagged 🔻 TASK-896, so all three doors behave the same). Dev (`SKIP_ADMIN_AUTH=true`) is unaffected.
**Tests (+5 in `service-auth-task896.test.ts`):** the list and `configuredServiceToken` cases (placeholder / empty / unset ⇒ undefined; a real token ⇒ itself); enforced + `SERVICE_TOKEN="change-me-service-token"` ⇒ 401 for no header, `X-Service-Token`, and Bearer, even when the caller presents that exact placeholder; a real token ⇒ 200 and the placeholder presented to it ⇒ 401; dev bypass passes with the placeholder configured; `adminOrService` refuses the placeholder as a machine token and accepts a real one. **Mutations 3/3 bite** (list emptied; `serviceAuth` reading the raw env; `adminOrService` reading the raw env).
**Evidence:** pinned tsc 5.9.3 → 0 `error TS` · backoffice-back suite 912 pass / 0 fail.
**⚠️ Deploy (carry to the note):** BB and SB must share the SAME real random `SERVICE_TOKEN`; the placeholder (still in both repos' `.env` / `.env.example`) now makes every machine call 401 in enforced mode, including SB's `fetchUnconfirmed` for the pay-confirm reminders (it will report `backoffice-unavailable`).
**Security review:** none open. This closes the "set but known" token. The comparison in `serviceAuth` / `adminOrService` is still a plain `!==` (as before; Bob's door uses `timingSafeEqual`): a timing attack on a network token is low value here and not changed in this TASK; say if you want them unified.
Ball: Sober (review).

## Jason (BE) 2026-11-09 — TASK-903 GREEN (fl_pay split per class type)
**Built (backoffice-back; no scheduler file, no git):**
- **Migration `0014_bo_pay_slip_line_class_type`** (hand-authored, journal idx 14, witness = the column): `bo.pay_slip_line.class_type bo.pay_class_type NULL`, additive, idempotent. Only `fl_pay` lines set it. Schema column `classType` added.
- **`pay-rules.ts` `flPayLines`:** one `AutoLine` per (FL date × class type with minutes AND a rate): `classType`, `sourceRef: fl:<date>:<class>`, label `"14/10 (Wed): private 2 Hr × 400"`, amount = that class's minutes ÷ 60 × rate rounded once at its own line. `missingRates` unchanged. `AutoLine` gains optional `classType`.
- **Stores:** both persist/return `classType` (sync inserts it; the 0011 index + per-class sourceRef make each class line idempotent). **`lineDTO` and `my-slip` lines gain `classType`** (null for every other kind), so it rides the overview-less slip read, `GET /slips/:id` and `GET /salary/my-slip`.
**§10 hand example re-worked (totals unchanged):** Ploy Wed 14/10 FL = **private 800.00 + group 250.00** (was one 1,050.00); Thu 15/10 private 2,000.00; Sat PT extra 350.00 → Ploy +3,400.00 as before. Ton unchanged.
**Evidence:** pinned tsc 5.9.3 → 0 `error TS` · backoffice-back suite 993 pass / 0 fail (+3 tests: a two-class day is two lines each with its classType/sourceRef/amount and they sum to the old 105,000; a double `runPeriod` adds nothing and the slip DTO carries `classType`; a vanished class drops only its own line while a new class adds one; plus my pinned DTO key sets updated: line keys now include `classType`, my-slip line shape too).
**Migration (F-038):** host check `DATABASE_URL host == 154.197.124.206 (sid)` → true before the apply; `applying` + `applied · verified` lines in MIGRATION-QUEUE naming the host; `drizzle-kit migrate` exit 0; `db:verify` Journal 15 · ledger 16 rows · Schema witnesses 13 applied · "every migration is recorded in the ledger AND witnessed in the schema"; ledger id 16, created_at 1794200000000.
**sid, rolled back (host check true; 8/8 PASS, left behind: periods 0, profiles 0):** the new column is `pay_class_type NULL`; a two-class day + a one-class day give 3 `fl_pay` lines with the right `classType` and amounts (800 + 250 + 2,000 = 3,050.00); raw rows carry `class_type` and `sourceRef = fl:<date>:<class>`; a second `runPeriod` changes nothing and the slip still has 3 lines; a duplicate per-class auto line through `insertLine` is a no-op returning the existing row; a non-fl_pay line has `classType` null.
**Follow-ons, as you noted (not done here):** the 879 export grouping fl_pay into "Freelance Teaching Pay · <Class>" sub-sections (it can read `classType`), and the FE render. **Existing rows** on sid keep `class_type` NULL and their old `fl:<date>` sourceRef; the next `runPeriod` of an open period removes those and writes the per-class ones (locked / confirmed slips are frozen and keep the old lines).

**Built (backoffice-back only; no Salary file, no git).** New `services/stock-write.service.ts` **reusing 904's store** (`StockStore` / `StockTx`: createProduct, updateProduct, addMovement, the Category tag and commission_group semantics): nothing duplicated. Three small, backward-compatible touches to Bob's code (all optional params, his tests/pins unchanged): `StockTx.addMovement` takes optional `refType` / `occurredOn` / `actor`, `StockTx.countKey?` (optional, for the stock-in idempotency), `NewProduct`/`ProductPatch` gain optional `active`; `dbStockStore` is now `makeDbStockStore(exec)` (same store, so a probe can pass a transaction); `listStockMovements(query, exec = db)`; the import pin `refType: "STOCK_IMPORT"` kept literally.
- **`POST /v1/stock/products` (CEO, 201):** `{code, name, category, priceSatang, commissionGroup, active, openingQuantity}` → a `bo.item` (`external_source=stock`, `external_ref=code`, INCOME, no ceiling, `unit_price_minor`, `commission_group` = the NAME only), the Category tag, and **ONE opening movement** (`ref_type STOCK_OPENING`, value 0, `occurred_on` = today, key `stock-product:<id>:opening`) **when `openingQuantity` > 0** (a 0 opening writes no movement: nothing moved). Returns `{id, code, name, category, priceSatang, quantity, commissionGroup:{name, amountSatang:null}|null, active}`. Duplicate code (case-insensitive, trimmed) ⇒ **409 `DUPLICATE_CODE`** `details:{code}`, nothing written (the unique index is also caught). Validation 400: price integer satang ≥ 0, opening whole ≥ 0, code ≤ 64, name ≤ 200, both non-empty. `STOCK_OPENING` reads as ADJUSTMENT in the movement report (like the import's opening).
- **`PATCH /v1/stock/products/:id` (CEO):** `{name?, priceSatang?, category?, commissionGroup?, active?}` → a **field edit, NO movement, balance untouched** (tested and checked on sid). 404 `UNKNOWN_ITEM`; empty or invalid patch 400; bad id 400.
- **`POST /v1/stock/in` (CEO + admin):** `{idempotencyKey, receivedOn, lines:[{itemId, qty>0}]}` → one **STOCK_IN movement per line** (+qty, `remaining_qty` raised in the same transaction, `occurred_on = receivedOn`, **no cost/price**), products locked FOR UPDATE first, idempotent on the key (`stock-in:<key>:<item>`; a re-POST writes nothing and answers `alreadyApplied`; a 23505 race re-reads). Response `{receiptId (= the key), movementsWritten, receivedOn, alreadyApplied?}`. Unknown item 404 / inactive 409 `ITEM_INACTIVE` refuse the WHOLE receipt (nothing written); `receivedOn` must be a real day and not after today (Bangkok); duplicate product in lines 400. Errors use the app's `{error:{code,message,details}}`.
- **`occurred_on`:** migration **`0017_bo_movement_occurred_on`** (`bo.movement.occurred_on date NULL`, additive, no back-fill, witness = the column). Stock IN sets it = `receivedOn`; **Stock OUT (909) now sets it = the Bangkok day of `soldAt`** (a one-line tagged change in `stock-out.service.ts`); the import sets it = today (explicit); the product opening = today. **`GET /v1/bo/stock/movements`** now returns `occurredOn` = `occurred_on`, falling back to the Bangkok day of `created_at`, **and the `from`/`to` window and the sort use that same business day** (so a back-dated receipt appears on its own day); FE contract unchanged.
**Evidence:** pinned tsc 5.9.3 → 0 `error TS` · backoffice-back suite 1087 pass / 0 fail (+16 in `stock-write-task911.test.ts`: create (opening movement, null opening, dup code, validation), edit (no movement, partial, 404/400), stock-in (movement per line, back-dated `occurred_on`, no price in the response, idempotent re-POST, atomic refusal, validation), `bangkokDate`, Stock OUT stamping the sale's Bangkok day, and the route gates with no database: product create/edit none 401 / coach 403 / admin 403, stock-in none 401 / coach 403 / admin and CEO reach validation 400, bad bodies 400).
**Migration (F-038):** host check true before the apply; `applying` + `applied · verified` lines in MIGRATION-QUEUE naming sid; `drizzle-kit migrate` exit 0; `db:verify` Journal 18 · ledger 19 rows · Schema witnesses 16 applied · "every migration is recorded in the ledger AND witnessed in the schema"; ledger id 19.
**sid, rolled back (host check true; 11/11 PASS; left behind 0 items, 0 movements):** a real create makes the item + category tag + commission group NAME + quantity 5, INCOME, no ceiling, ONE `STOCK_OPENING` movement; duplicate code 409; edit changes fields and writes no movement; stock-in written, re-POST `alreadyApplied`, balance 5 + 7 = 12 with only 2 movements; **the report (`listStockMovements`) shows the back-dated receipt on 2026-11-05 and the opening on 2026-11-09, the date window filters on the business day, and a legacy row with NULL `occurred_on` falls back to the Bangkok day of `created_at`**.
**Security review:** none open. New writes are CEO-only (product create/edit) or CEO+admin (stock in: receiving only adds, no pay/revenue); bodies are zod-validated and bounded (qty ≤ 1,000,000, 200 lines, code/name lengths, uuid ids, key pattern); all SQL via Drizzle (the one raw fragment, the report's day expression, takes bound `from`/`to`); no cost or price in the Stock IN response; the idempotency key is client-supplied but only keys inserts (unique index), it cannot read another receipt. Residual, by design: an admin can add stock (the contract allows it) and the receipt is attributed to them in `actor`.
**Questions:** (1) A zero `openingQuantity` writes no opening movement: OK, or do you want a 0-qty row? (2) `receiptId` = the idempotency key (no separate receipts table; a table would need a migration): fine for 912? (3) The product create returns 201 with the product (your pin said only "creates"): shout if you want 200.

## Jason (BE) 2026-11-09 — TASK-915 STEP-0 (read-only; nothing built, no schema yet) — check-in with design questions
**Read-only everything:** I opened the customer workbook and the final-UI file, and ran two SELECTs on sid (host check true; counts only). No write, no migration, no code.
### A. The Demo tab (`SOM-BALANCE-Backoffice-spec/data/customer-files/Format-Stock-2026-from-feen-2026-10-30.xlsx`, sheet `Demo`, 163 rows × 9 cols; the other sheet is `Balance Stock`)
- **Shape:** row 1 = a merged group header `C1:H1` "Location/ Balance"; row 2 = `Category | Name | Bravo | STA | (blank) | RIS | ABC | Abbey | Remark`; data from row 3. **Column E has NO header** (59 cells filled, all 0). No Model Code column at all (Balance Stock has one; Demo has only Category + Name).
- **Data:** 155 data rows (6 rows with a blank name are spacers), **23 categories** in vertically merged cells (`A3:A11`, `A12:A15`, … 21 merges; exceljs gives the master value on each row), 0 formulas, 0 non-numeric cells, 0 negatives, blanks = not counted. Per location (sum / non-zero cells): **Bravo 1,172 / 134 · STA 70 / 24 · E 0 / 0 · RIS 6 / 4 · ABC 0 / 0 · Abbey 22 / 10** ⇒ **172 non-zero (item, site) balances** to seed. 13 rows carry a Thai `Remark` (col I).
- **Traps a reader must handle:** category text has stray whitespace/newlines (`"\nHelmet"`, `"Inlineskate\n(Decahtlon)"`, `" Protection Pad"`); **4 item names are rich-text cells** (rows 43–46, `FUN NEW 7.75''` …) that a plain reader prints as `[object Object]` (904's `cellValue` already joins `richText`); **duplicate names**: `สนับ / ชุด -Red+Black` ×2 (rows 9, 10), `รอครูนับ` ("waiting for the teacher to count") ×2.
- **Mapping to the sale catalogue: impossible by code, and by name 0 of 155 match** the Balance Stock names (I compared normalised names). **sid has 0 `bo.item` stock products right now** (904's import has not been applied there) and **no `bo.demo_*` tables**.
### B. The final-UI Demo screen (`final-ui/src/pages/b/stock/Demo.tsx`, 278 lines; mock `mock/b-stock.ts:68-97`)
- A table **items × sites + a Total column** (`:140-165`), then a **History** table: When (date only, no time) · Who · Item · From → To · Qty · Reason (`:169`).
- **Actions:** `Move A → B` (reason REQUIRED, `doMove :48`, refuses qty > have at A), `Delete` (reason required, `:59`; to = "Deleted"; "removes the pieces from the table, but the history is kept"), `Defect Product → Demo` (More menu, reason required, `:68`: +qty at a site, from = "Defect Product"), `Add Location` (`:76`, appended last), `Location Order` (`:85`, ▲▼; **Bravo is leftmost, order is settable**). Mock sites: `Bravo, STA, ABC, RIS, WA (Abbey), KIS`.
- **Not on this screen:** any sale→demo / demo→sale dialog (they exist only as mock history rows: "Sale stock → Bravo", "Demo → Sale stock"), and any create/edit of items or categories.
### C. Reuse boundary (`bo.item` / `bo.movement`, 904 + 911)
Reusable as-is: the signed-qty + stored-balance-in-one-transaction pattern, `refuseIfNegative`, idempotency keys, `occurred_on` (0017), `makeDbStockStore` style exec injection for rolled-back probes. Demo's own tables are right (separate balances, own history).
### D. DESIGN QUESTIONS before I cut the schema (recommendation first)
1. **What is a demo "item"?** The sheet has Category + Name only and nothing matches the sale catalogue. **Recommend a separate `bo.demo_item(id, category, name, remark, active, sale_item_id NULL → bo.item)`**, seeded from the sheet; the two sale↔demo bridges take BOTH ids (the demo item and the sale `itemId`) and remember the pairing in `sale_item_id` to pre-fill next time. Alternative (reuse `bo.item` with `external_source='demo'`) would put demo items in the sale item list and the Category tag, so I advise against it. OK?
2. **Column E (blank header, all zeros) = KIS?** The REQ and the FE list KIS as a site and the sheet has exactly one unnamed location column; I would seed the six sites in the FE's order (`Bravo, STA, ABC, RIS, WA (Abbey), KIS`) and treat E as KIS (all 0s, so nothing depends on it). Please have Porter confirm with the customer.
3. **Seed duplicates / spacers:** skip the 6 blank-name rows; keep the two `สนับ / ชุด -Red+Black` rows and the two `รอครูนับ` rows as separate items (one row each, by sheet order; no unique-name constraint) rather than merge or drop, and keep the 13 remarks. Trim category whitespace. OK, or merge?
4. **"Defect Product → Demo" (FE) vs "broken sale goods → Demo" (REQ):** the FE just adds stock at a demo site from "Defect Product". Does it ALSO take the piece out of sale stock (my design: `BROKEN_FROM_SALE` = sale −qty + demo +qty, reason required)? If the defect piece was never counted in sale stock, it should be a plain demo +qty. Which?
5. **Who triggers sale→demo and demo→sale?** No screen has a button for them. I will build the two endpoints as designed; tell me if a later FE screen (Stock OUT / product row) will call them, or if they can wait.
6. **Movement-report vocabulary:** 904's report types are `SALE · STOCK_IN · ADJUSTMENT · FROM_DEMO` (the FE's final labels). The TASK adds a sale-side `TO_DEMO`; that is a new label the FE does not have. Either the FE adds "To Demo", or I report sale→demo as `ADJUSTMENT` (ref_type `TO_DEMO`) and keep the contract unchanged. Which?
7. **Two small additions the FE needs that the TASK does not list:** `POST /v1/demo/sites {name}` (Add Location, unique name) and `PUT /v1/demo/sites/order {ids[]}` with a `sort_order` column on `bo.demo_site` (Location Order; Bravo first by default). Items/categories "editable" (REQ) has no screen yet; I would add `POST/PATCH /v1/demo/items` after the foundation. OK to include sites add/order now?
8. **Who:** the history's "Who" shows a person (Ploy, Ton); I store `actor` = the token's username (`sub`). Fine?
**My plan after your answers (no more questions needed):** 2 migrations (0018 `demo_site`+`demo_item`+`demo_stock`+`demo_movement`; seed as an idempotent script, not an API), reads (`/demo/sites`, `/demo/stock`, `/demo/movements`), writes (`move`, `from-sale`, `to-sale`, `write-off`, + sites add/order per Q7), tests, sid rolled-back check, `/security-review`. Size ≈ 2 days.

## Jason (BE) 2026-11-09 — TASK-911 revenue slice DONE (`revenueCategoryId` on the product form)
**Built (backoffice-back; no new migration: your 0018 column is already on sid):** `POST /v1/stock/products` now **requires `revenueCategoryId`** (a uuid; missing / not a uuid ⇒ 400 `VALIDATION`), and `PATCH /v1/stock/products/:id` accepts an optional `revenueCategoryId` (a uuid; **never null**, so a product can change category but cannot lose it). The service checks it against Bob's existing `tx.revenueCategories()` (reused, not duplicated) and **the picker's own set, i.e. the same filter as `GET /v1/stock/revenue-categories`: ACTIVE and type SALES / FNB / RENT / OTHER**; anything else (unknown id, inactive, or a COURSE / TRIAL / VOUCHER / ECA category that is not offered) ⇒ **400 `UNKNOWN_REVENUE_CATEGORY`** `details:{field:"revenueCategoryId"}`, nothing written. The id is stored through the same `createProduct` / `updateProduct`, and the product DTO now returns `revenueCategoryId`. So a product made on the form is never born un-sellable.

## Jason (BE) 2026-11-09 — TASK-915 GREEN (Stock Demo foundation) — to Sober
**Built to your answers + DECISIONS row 22 (backoffice-back only; no sale-side change, no bridge, no TO_DEMO, no alerts, 15b out, no money, no git).**
- **Schema (`schema-demo.ts` + `0019_bo_demo_stock`, 4 NEW tables, additive, witness = the last index):** `bo.demo_site(name unique-ci, sort_order, active)` · `bo.demo_item(category, name, remark, active, sale_item_id NULL → bo.item, seed_row, sort_order)` · `bo.demo_stock(demo_item_id, site_id, qty ≥ 0)` PK(item, site) · `bo.demo_movement(demo_item_id, type MOVE|DEFECT_IN|WRITE_OFF|OPENING, qty > 0, from_site_id, to_site_id, reason, actor, occurred_on, idempotency_key UNIQUE)`. DB CHECKs refuse qty < 0 and a zero-qty movement (proven on sid, 23514). History is never updated or deleted.
- **Reads (`/v1/demo`, CEO + admin, no money):** `GET /sites` · `GET /stock?q=&category=` (the matrix the screen draws: sites left to right + per ACTIVE item `balances{siteId:qty}` + `total`) · `GET /movements?from&to&siteId&type&q&limit` (who · date · item · from → to · qty · reason; window on `occurred_on`, inclusive, ≤ 366 days; from = «Defect Product» for DEFECT_IN, to = «Deleted» for WRITE_OFF).
- **Writes (CEO + admin; each ONE transaction, item row locked FOR UPDATE, idempotent on `key`, `occurredOn` default today (Bangkok), never future; 409 `NEGATIVE_BALANCE` reuses 904's `refuseIfNegative`; errors in the app envelope):** `POST /move` (reason required) · `POST /defect-in` (plain +qty at a site, reason required, nothing leaves sale stock) · `POST /delete` (write-off, reason required: with `siteId`+`qty` = that many off that site [this is what the FE's Delete dialog sends: Item / At / Qty / Reason]; **without them = zero every site (one WRITE_OFF each) and deactivate the item**, your Q7 reading, so both behaviours exist) · `POST /sites {name}` (201, 409 `DUPLICATE_SITE`) · `PUT /sites/order {ids[]}` (exactly the active sites). Responses `{occurredOn, balances:{siteId:qty}, movementsWritten, alreadyApplied?}` so the screen refreshes without a re-read.
- **Seed (`bun run db:seed-demo-stock [--file] [--dry]`, idempotent, one transaction):** the 6 sites in the FE order; one `demo_item` per named sheet row (6 spacers skipped, the duplicate-name rows kept as separate items, remarks kept, category whitespace/newlines tidied, rich-text names joined through 904's `cellValue`, now exported); **column E ignored (row 22), KIS starts empty**; one `OPENING` movement + stored balance per non-zero cell (key `demo-seed:<row>:<site>`) so the ledger still sums to the balance (the one type beyond your three: it is seed-only, no endpoint writes it).
**Evidence:** pinned tsc 5.9.3 → 0 `error TS` · backoffice-back suite 1129 pass / 0 fail (+17 in `demo-stock-task915.test.ts`: the sheet parser incl. spacers, duplicates, E ignored, bad cells; move atomic + history row + back-dating + idempotent; negative refused with nothing written; reason required; defect-in; delete partial and whole + history kept + repeat; sites add/order; and every route none 401 / coach 403, admin and ceo reach validation 400). **Migration (F-038):** host check true; `applying` + `applied · verified` lines in MIGRATION-QUEUE; db:verify Journal 20 · ledger 21 · witnesses 18, "every migration is recorded and witnessed". ⚠️ **One thing worth knowing for every BE:** my first `drizzle-kit migrate` printed success but did NOT create the tables: my journal `when` (1794600000000) equalled Bob's 0018 `when`, and drizzle only applies migrations strictly newer than the ledger's newest (the ledger row it wrote was Bob's). Bumping mine to 1794700000000 and re-running fixed it. Journal `when` values must be unique across the team; `db:verify` caught it ("NOT recorded").
**sid, rolled back (host check true; 24/24 PASS, left behind 0 sites / 0 items / 0 moves):** the real seed (6 sites, 155 items, 172 balances, 1,270 units = 1,172 + 70 + 6 + 22) and a second seed adding nothing; sites in order; matrix totals; KIS empty; move/idempotent/negative/no-reason; defect-in; partial and whole delete (item deactivated, leaves the matrix, history keeps MOVE + DEFECT_IN + OPENING + WRITE_OFF); the history shows who / «Defect Product» / «Deleted»; site filter; add site, duplicate 409, reorder; the table CHECK refusing a negative. **Then I ran the REAL seed on sid** (host-checked; 6 sites, 155 items, 172 balances, 1,270 units) and a `--dry` re-run added nothing, so Fanta's Demo screen has real data to build on.
**Security review:** none open. Routes are CEO + admin only (no pay, revenue or money exists in these tables; 401 / 403 tested for every route); all bodies and queries are zod-bounded (uuids, qty 1–1,000,000, reason ≤ 500, key pattern with no `:` so the key prefix cannot collide, ≤ 366-day window, limit ≤ 500); all SQL via Drizzle (LIKE terms escaped); a reason is stored and returned as plain text (the FE renders it as text); `actor` = the token's `sub`; the seed is a CLI script, not an endpoint. Residual, by design: an admin can move / write off demo stock (the screen is CEO + Admin).
**Not built (as agreed):** sale↔demo bridge, `TO_DEMO`, items/categories create/edit (`POST/PATCH /v1/demo/items`, the thin fast follow-on you described: say if you want it next), alerts, 15b.
Ball: Sober (review). I am free for the next item (blocked on nobody).

## Jason (BE) 2026-11-09 — TASK-920 GREEN (Salary notification events, NO money in any text) — for Silver's review
**Built (backoffice-back; no migration, no git):** new `src/services/salary-notify.ts` (the text builders, the keys, `realNotify`, `fire`, `notifyCommission`) + one tagged `🔻 TASK-920` call site at each write point, all through the accepted 917 seam (`notifyAssigned` → `recipientsForType` + `createNotification`). **No change to any line amount, key or response shape**: the only addition is a `notifyFailed` counter that appears **only when > 0**.
| type | where | fires when |
|---|---|---|
| Bonus | `bonus-lines.service.ts` `applyBonusLines` | each line **written** (not `unchanged` / `changed` / `orphaned`) |
| Transportation | `transport.service.ts` `applyTransportLines` | each line **written** |
| Extra Hour | `salary.service.ts` `runPeriod` | each extra-hour line **inserted** by the run (a kept or updated line, FL pay and every re-run: nothing). `syncAutoLines` now also returns the lines it inserted; `AutoLine` gains `extraMinutes` so the text says "2 Hr" without parsing the label (which carries the rate) |
| OT | `addOt` (`POST /slips/:id/ot`) | the OT line written |
| Other | `addLine` (`POST /slips/:id/lines`) **and** `addEntry` (`POST /entries`, kind `other`) | the line written; **Merit and FL pay emit nothing** |
| Commission | none exists in BB | **`notifyCommission(notify, {lineId, coach, day?, detail?})` is exported** for the future writer (key `pay-line:<lineId>`; a detail that could hold money is dropped) |
**Text (no amount, ever):** `<Type> · <coach> · <dd/mm/yy>` + one detail: Bonus = «1st Trial» (b50) / «Renew» (b100); Transportation = `<school> · Round trip|One way` (no km, no vehicle: they give the pay away); Extra Hour / OT = `<n> Hr`; Other = the reason **as typed unless it could hold money, then «Other item»**. **My rule is blunter than your example: ANY digit, a currency symbol (฿ $ € £) or a currency word (บาท, สตางค์, baht, satang, THB) drops the reason.** A false positive only costs the detail ("2 helmets" becomes «Other item»); say if you want it looser. Coach name from the store's `teacherNames`, the word «coach» when unknown. Dates: Bonus and Other use the run/entry day (no line date), Transportation the trip date, Extra Hour the day, OT the OT date.
**Keys / failure:** auto line `pay:<slipId>:<kind>:<sourceRef>`, manual line `pay-line:<lineId>`, so a re-run or a double click cannot notify twice. The pay line is **committed first**; the notification runs after, inside `fire()` which **never throws** (a thrown or failed notifier = one counted failure, logged without text or recipient). Producers default to notifications OFF when no notifier is injected (every existing test), ON in production wiring (`createSalaryService` default deps, `applyBonusLinesFor`, `transportServiceFor`).
**Evidence:** pinned tsc 5.9.3 → 0 `error TS` · backoffice-back suite 1191 pass / 0 fail · new `salary-notify-task920.test.ts` (20 tests: text builders; the no-money rule incl. the Other-reason cases and a property check against the line amounts in every producer; Extra Hour one per inserted line, re-run nothing, a new day just that one, a changed day nothing, FL pay nothing; OT / Other (CEO) / Other (admin entry, response keys unchanged) / Merit nothing; a refused (locked) write emits nothing; Bonus 1st Trial / Renew, re-run nothing; Transportation text without km/vehicle/amount; failure and **thrown** notifier leave the lines written and count `notifyFailed`; Commission helper). **Mutation set 14/14 bites** (no-money filter digits / currency words, both keys, Bonus detail, Round trip / One way, a thrown notifier escaping, the Extra Hour / OT / Bonus / Transportation type, Extra Hour notifying existing lines, Merit notifying, transport text carrying km). **sid, rolled back (host .206 checked; 9/9 PASS, left behind: 0 periods, 0 notifications):** a real teacher + the seeded CEO: an OT line, an Other line whose reason held "1,500", a Bonus 50 line and an Extra Hour line produced exactly four feed items for the CEO (`OT · coach · 20/07/98 · 3 Hr`, `Extra Hour · … · 2 Hr`, `Bonus · … · 1st Trial`, `Other · … · Other item`), no text with a money figure; a re-run of the pay run and the bonus apply added none; a failing notifier gave `notifyFailed: 1` with the OT line still written.
**Security review (data exposure: the no-money rule):** none open. Texts are built only from fixed words, the coach's display name, a date, an hours count, a school name and (Other) a reason that is dropped when it holds any digit or currency token; no amount, km, vehicle, rate or base ever reaches a text builder's input (the builders do not accept one). Recipients come only from the assignments table (the CEO role today); an admin sees the same items. A notification failure cannot change pay (committed first, caught, counted). Residual, by design: a free-text reason without digits can still say "bonus for Pete" in words; the CEO enters it, and it is the CEO's own wording.
**Notes for Silver:** (1) Recipients today are the CEO role only (917's seed); London's Low-Stock assignment does not receive Salary types. (2) The `Other` entry by an admin also notifies the CEO. (3) Commission has no writer yet; the helper is ready.
Ball: Silver (review) · Sober (FYI).
